# Chapter 1 — Reunion at a familiar house

## English editing copy

Exported from the current game on 4 October 2026. Includes every branch of the currently playable Chapter 1.

This is an editing copy; changing it does not change the game automatically. Keep scene and line IDs when editing so revisions can be matched reliably. Scene sections follow source order; use the “Next” and choice destinations to follow a particular route. Alternative branches are not all played in a single run.

Narration has no speaker label. MC’s silent thoughts are italicized. Spoken dialogue has a speaker label. Bracketed notes are implementation directions. Placeholders such as {name} remain for player customization. English narration shared by both modes is intentionally repeated. These are the translations currently used by the game, including any existing wording issues or fallback text, rather than a newly rewritten translation.

## 01 / The road back [journey]

[Background: bus; time: day.]

[Audio direction: "bus".]

[Existing CG: way-back. Begins at the line containing “The sea appears”; ends at the line containing “SAINT LUIS!”.]

[journey:0]

It’s been eight hours since we left the city, including the stopover. I can’t get comfortable anymore.

[journey:1]

I move my knee away from the seat in front of me. The window keeps rattling beside my head.

[journey:2]

I grew up in Saint Luis with Lola nearby.

[journey:3]

My parents took me to Manila when I was eleven.

[journey:4]

I kept hoping I’d come back the next year. I never did.

[journey:5]

Twelve years have passed. I’m twenty-three now.

[journey:6]

The sea appears between two buildings. I lean closer to the window to get a better look.

[journey:7]

*I’m finally back in Saint Luis.*

[journey:8]

There are small fishing boats in the bay. I can see the old baywalk along the shore.

[journey:9]

Lola used to take Rowan and me for walks there in the afternoons.

[journey:10]

She held our hands when we crossed the road. Me on one side, Rowan on the other.

[journey:11]

He’d complain that we weren’t little anymore. Then keep holding on after we’d crossed.

[journey:12]

**MC:** I wonder how he’s been.

[journey:13]

Thinking about him makes me smile.

[journey:14]

Then I remember Lola’s funeral.

[journey:15]

*He was probably at the funeral.*

[journey:16]

Two years ago, Lola died during finals week.

[journey:17]

My parents said I couldn’t miss my exams.

[journey:18]

I wanted to argue. Instead, I asked if we could go afterward.

[journey:19]

“We’ll talk about it.”

[journey:20]

We never did.

[journey:21]

I still took the exams. Handed everything in on time.

[journey:22]

*I was her grandchild, and I wasn’t there.*

[journey:23]

This trip is different.

[journey:24]

I paid for the ticket with money I’d saved.

[journey:25]

I told my parents I was leaving. I didn’t ask.

[journey:26]

Then I left before we could finish another argument.

[journey:27]

I needed a break from college. From home. From having the same conversation in my head.

[journey:28]

Lola’s house needs cleaning. A few things probably need fixing.

[journey:29]

*I can do that.*

[journey:30]

*Sweep a floor. Fix a window. Get some sleep.*

[journey:31]

*That was the plan.*

[journey:32]

I look back toward the baywalk.

[journey:33]

*What am I going to say if I see Rowan?*

[journey:34]

*Sorry I stopped calling?*

[journey:35]

*Sorry I believed you’d forgotten me?*

[journey:36]

**Conductor:** SAINT LUIS! SAINT LUIS!

[journey:37]

The shout makes me jump. It wakes the passenger across the aisle too.

[journey:38]

**MC:** Whoa.

[journey:39]

*At least I’m not the only one he scared.*

**Next:** busBump.

## A very good start [busBump]

[Background: bus; time: day.]

[Audio direction: "bus".]

[busBump:0]

Everyone gets up at once. After eight hours, we’re all ready to get off this bus.

[busBump:1]

I reach for my bag.

[busBump:2]

A man bumps into me. My hand slips off the seat, and I fall into the aisle.

[busBump:3]

**MC:** Ow!

[busBump:4]

I sit on the floor for a moment. My hip hurts, and my bag is still caught around my wrist.

[busBump:5]

**Stranger:** Sorry! Sorry. My son’s already getting off.

[busBump:6]

He looks toward the front of the bus.

[busBump:7]

**Stranger:** I need to catch him.

**Next:** wallet.

## A very good start [wallet]

[Background: bus; time: day.]

[Audio direction: "bus".]

[wallet:0]

I brush the dirt off my clothes.

[wallet:1]

**MC:** Great start.

[wallet:2]

I check that I still have my bag and phone. Then I reach for my wallet.

[wallet:3]

I check both back pockets.

[wallet:4]

They’re empty.

[wallet:5]

I search the seat, the floor underneath it, and the aisle.

[wallet:6]

**MC:** No. Come on.

[wallet:7]

My wallet is gone.

[wallet:8]

I look toward the exit. The man is gone too.

[wallet:9]

*Did he take it when he bumped into me? Was there even a child with him?*

[wallet:10]

**MC:** Shit.

[wallet:11]

My cards and IDs are in my bag. I check them twice anyway.

[wallet:12]

The wallet only had cash.

[wallet:13]

But I needed that cash to pay for the ride to Lola’s house.

[wallet:14]

Through the windshield, I can see the road toward town.

[wallet:15]

The nearest ATM is in town, too far to walk with my bag. Lola’s house is the other way.

[wallet:16]

**MC:** Just my luck.

[wallet:17]

I hold onto my bag and try to think of what to do.

[wallet:18]

Then I remember that some buses pass through Santiago Street, near Lola’s house.

[wallet:19]

*Maybe this one does. I should ask.*

**Next:** driver.

## A small kindness [driver]

[Background: bus; time: day.]

[Audio direction: "bus".]

[driver:0]

**MC:** Excuse me, sir?

[driver:1]

The driver looks over.

[driver:2]

**MC:** Will you be passing through Santiago Street?

[driver:3]

**Driver:** Yes. Is that where you’re getting off?

[driver:4]

**MC:** Yes, sir.

[driver:5]

I feel a little better. At least the bus goes where I need to go.

[driver:6]

**MC:** Thank you.

[driver:7]

Then I notice the tickets in the conductor’s hand.

[driver:8]

*Will I have to pay extra to stay on? I don’t have any cash left.*

[driver:9]

I briefly think about getting off before they ask me to pay. But then I’d still be stuck here.

[driver:10]

**MC:** Is there an extra fare? Because—

[driver:11]

**Driver:** Take a seat. I won’t charge you extra.

[driver:12]

Before I can explain, he points toward where I fell.

[driver:13]

**Driver:** I saw someone bump into you. Did you lose something?

[driver:14]

**MC:** My wallet, sir.

[driver:15]

The driver sighs.

[driver:16]

**Driver:** All right. I’ll drop you off at Santiago.

[driver:17]

**MC:** Thank you. Really.

[driver:18]

He points to an empty seat.

[driver:19]

**Driver:** Keep a good hold on your bag.

[driver:20]

**MC:** Yes, sir.

[driver:21]

I sit down and keep my bag on my lap as the bus starts moving again.

[driver:22]

I watch the houses pass by the window.

[driver:23]

I’m still upset about my wallet.

[driver:24]

But I don’t have to figure out the rest of the trip standing in the aisle.

[driver:25]

*For now, I can sit.*

**Next:** hill.

## The hill [hill]

[Background: saint-luis; time: day.]

[Audio direction: "home".]

[Existing CG: hill-houses. Begins at the line containing “I can see his house”; ends according to the scene transition.]

[hill:0]

**MC:** Here, please. Thank you!

[hill:1]

**Driver:** Take care.

[hill:2]

I wait until the bus has gone. Then I turn toward the hill.

[hill:3]

It looks smaller.

[hill:4]

My legs disagree almost immediately.

[hill:5]

Halfway up, I have to change hands with my bag.

[hill:6]

Rowan and I used to race here. Downhill was easy.

[hill:7]

Uphill, one of us always started arguing about where the finish line was.

[hill:8]

I can see his house now. And beyond it, Lola’s porch.

[hill:9]

After we’d worn ourselves out, she’d call us in for bibingka.

[hill:10]

We’d eat before it had cooled properly. Then go straight back to hide-and-seek.

[hill:11]

I remember those afternoons so clearly.

[hill:12]

The heat. The crumbs. Trying not to laugh when Rowan walked past my hiding place.

[hill:13]

*I can’t remember the last thing I said to her.*

[hill:14]

My grip tightens around the bag. I try.

[hill:15]

*Something ordinary, probably.*

[hill:16]

*Something I thought I’d get to follow up on.*

[hill:17]

The path blurs.

[hillCry:6]

## The other door [otherDoor]

[Background: saint-luis; time: day.]

[Audio direction: "home".]

[otherDoor:0]

*The house won’t clean itself.*

[otherDoor:1]

That sounds like something Lola would have said. I almost laugh.

[otherDoor:2]

Rowan’s house is right there.

[otherDoor:3]

The nameplate says Mayumi. The paint around it looks newer than the rest of the gate.

[otherDoor:4]

*I could knock. Just to let them know I’m back.*

[otherDoor:5]

*That would be normal. Polite, even.*

[otherDoor:6]

I rehearse it.

[otherDoor:7]

*Hi. It’s been a while.*

[otherDoor:8]

*Twelve years is probably longer than “a while.”*

[otherDoor:9]

*What if he opens the door?*

[otherDoor:10]

*What if he looks happy to see me?*

[otherDoor:11]

*Somehow, I haven’t prepared for that either.*

[otherDoor:12]

**MC:** I can say hello.

[otherDoor:13]

**MC:** In theory.

## The other door [neighborVisit]

[Background: saint-luis; time: day.]

[Audio direction: "home".]

[neighborVisit:0]

I turn toward the porch before I can hold a full debate about it.

[neighborVisit:1]

Up close, the door is familiar enough to make my stomach tighten.

[neighborVisit:2]

*How many afternoons did I spend waiting here?*

[neighborVisit:3]

I knock.

[neighborVisit:4]

Nothing. I listen.

[neighborVisit:5]

Then knock again, a little louder.

[neighborVisit:6]

**MC:** Tita Mayumi? Hello?

[neighborVisit:7]

I hesitate before calling for him.

[neighborVisit:8]

**MC:** Rowan?

[neighborVisit:9]

I wait longer than I need to.

[neighborVisit:10]

**MC:** Okay. Nobody home.

[neighborVisit:11]

The relief is immediate. The disappointment takes another second.

[neighborVisit:12]

I step off the porch and head toward Lola’s house.

[Background: saint-luis; time: day.]

[Audio direction: "home".]

[neighborWait:0]

I keep walking.

[neighborWait:1]

## A familiar stranger [doorRepair]

[Background: repair; time: day.]

[Audio direction: "quiet".]

[doorRepair:0]

I stop.

[doorRepair:1]

Another tap. Then a scrape.

[doorRepair:2]

Someone’s at the house.

[doorRepair:3]

**MC:** What the hell?

[doorRepair:4]

I hurry the last few steps.

[doorRepair:5]

A man about my age is kneeling in front of the door, his back toward me.

[doorRepair:6]

A screwdriver lies beside his knee. He has a hammer in one hand.

[doorRepair:7]

The door is open just far enough that I can’t see what he’s doing to it.

[doorRepair:8]

My bag knocks against my leg as I climb onto the porch.

[doorRepair:9]

**MC:** Excuse me!

[doorRepair:10]

He stops.

[doorRepair:11]

**MC:** What are you doing? That’s my lola’s house.

[doorRepair:12]

The hammer lowers.

[doorRepair:13]

The man turns.

## Welcome back [rHug]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day.]

[Music: reunion.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[Existing CG: reunion-hug. Begins at the line containing “He wraps”; ends according to the scene transition.]

[rHug:5]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day.]

[Music: reunion.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[rSpace:0]

[rSpace:1 · Expression: neutral]

[rSpace:2]

[rSpace:3]

He stays beside me without trying again.

[rSpace:4 · Expression: smile]

[rSpace:5]

[rSpace:6]

[rSpace:7 · Expression: playful]

[rSpace:8]

[rSpace:9]

[rSpace:10]

**Next:** rEmotion.

## Take your time [rCry]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day.]

[Music: reunion.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[rCry:0 · Expression: concerned]

*Shit. Why now?*

[rCry:1]

I wipe at my face, but that only makes it more obvious.

[rCry:2]

**Rowan:** Hey.

[rCry:3 · Only if reunionBoundary = "hug"]

He loosens his arms so I can move away if I want.

[rCry:4]

**MC:** Sorry. I didn’t mean to—

[rCry:5]

**Rowan:** You don’t have to be sorry.

[rCry:6]

**Rowan:** It’s okay.

[rCry:7]

I try to answer. Nothing comes out.

[rCry:8]

He waits.

[rCry:9]

*I don’t have to explain it right away.*

[rCry:10]

*For once, nobody is asking me to pull myself together.*

[rCry:11 · Only if reunionBoundary = "hug"]

I lean against him again. He holds me without saying anything else.

[rCry:12 · Only if reunionBoundary = "space"]

He stays close, leaving enough room between us.

[rCry:13]

After a while, I take a steadier breath.

[rCry:14]

**MC:** Thanks, Ro.

[rCry:15 · Expression: smile]

**Rowan:** Yeah.

**Next:** rCatchup.

## Twelve years to catch up on [rCatchup]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[rCatchup:0 · Expression: smile]

**MC:** So. Long time no see.

[rCatchup:1]

**Rowan:** Yeah. Just a little.

[rCatchup:2]

**MC:** Twelve years, Ro.

[rCatchup:3 · Expression: playful]

**Rowan:** I was trying to be polite.

[rCatchup:4]

I laugh.

[rCatchup:5]

It helps.

[rCatchup:6 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** How long are you staying?

[rCatchup:7]

**MC:** For the summer.

[rCatchup:8 · Expression: surprised]

His eyebrows lift.

[rCatchup:9]

**Rowan:** The whole summer?

[rCatchup:10]

**MC:** That’s the plan.

[rCatchup:11]

**MC:** You seem pleased.

[rCatchup:12 · Expression: playful]

**Rowan:** I’m being very normal about it.

[rCatchup:13]

*I’m glad he isn’t.*

[rCatchup:14]

**MC:** I needed a break from college.

[rCatchup:15 · Expression: smile]

I look toward the open door.

[rCatchup:16]

**MC:** And I thought I’d clean the house. See what needs fixing.

[rCatchup:17]

**Rowan:** There’s a list.

[rCatchup:18]

**MC:** Of course there is.

[rCatchup:19]

**Rowan:** You don’t have to start today.

[rCatchup:20]

**MC:** Good. Today I’d like to sit down.

[rCatchup:21 · Expression: playful]

**Rowan:** Very ambitious.

[rCatchup:22]

For a few minutes, talking to him feels easy.

[rCatchup:23]

He asks about the trip. I tell him about the crowded bus and my missing wallet.

[rCatchup:24 · Expression: concerned]

**Rowan:** Someone took it?

[rCatchup:25]

**MC:** I think so. My cards were in my bag, at least.

[rCatchup:26]

**Rowan:** Do you need anything?

[rCatchup:27]

**MC:** I’m okay for now. The driver helped me get here.

[rCatchup:28]

**Rowan:** If that changes, tell me.

[rCatchup:29]

He nods, though he still looks concerned.

At first, we did both. Calls and letters.

[rLetterYears:2]

“Not now.” “Maybe next weekend.”

[rLetterYears:5]

[rLetterYears:6]

[rLetterYears:7]

## Two years earlier [rBedroom]

[Background: manila-bedroom; time: night.]

[Music: rainy-night.]

[Rowan sprite: hidden; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[rBedroom:0]

Lola had died a few days earlier.

[rBedroom:1]

I was in my room, staring at my phone.

[rBedroom:2]

I kept opening her contact.

[rBedroom:3]

There was no reason to. I knew she wouldn’t answer.

[rBedroom:4]

Downstairs, my parents were talking.

[rBedroom:5]

They sounded normal.

[rBedroom:6]

I knew people didn’t have to cry all the time to be grieving.

[rBedroom:7]

*Still, I couldn’t understand how they could sound so normal.*

[rBedroom:8]

*I wanted to go back to Saint Luis.*

[rBedroom:9]

*Even if I was already too late for the funeral.*

[rBedroom:10]

I stood up before I could talk myself out of asking.

**Next:** rLanding.

## What they kept from me [rLanding]

[Background: manila-landing; time: night.]

[Music: rainy-night.]

[Rowan sprite: hidden; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[rLanding:0]

**Dad:** What are we doing with the house?

[rLanding:1]

**Mom:** Not much we can do until someone checks the repairs.

[rLanding:2]

**Dad:** Could rent it out.

[rLanding:3]

**Mom:** With what money? The roof alone will cost us.

[rLanding:4]

I stop near the stairs.

[rLanding:5]

**Mom:** Rowan’s been looking after it. Let him handle it for now.

[rLanding:6]

**Dad:** After the way he spoke to us?

[rLanding:7]

**Mom:** He was upset.

[rLanding:8]

**Dad:** He kept asking why we hadn’t brought {name}. Like we owed him an explanation.

[rLanding:9]

I take another step down.

[rLanding:10]

**Mom:** And he asked about his number again.

[rLanding:11]

**Dad:** You didn’t give it to {name}, did you?

[rLanding:12]

**Mom:** No.

[rLanding:13]

For a second, I can only hear the rain.

[rLanding:14]

**MC:** Why not?

[rLanding:15]

They both look up.

[rLanding:16]

**Mom:** How long have you been standing there?

Neither of them answers.

I lift the lid.

I looked for yellow envelopes after every visit.

[rLetters:23]

**MC:** The letters I gave you. Did you deliver them?

[rLetters:26]

[rLetters:27]

I reach for the others.

Mom pulls the box toward her.

[Background: manila-service; time: night.]

[Rowan sprite: hidden; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[rBurning:1]

[rBurning:2]

I hold Rowan’s envelope against my chest.

[rBurning:6]

Dad stands up.

[rBurning:7]

Mom comes over and puts an arm around my shoulders.

[rBurning:8]

I go stiff.

[rBurning:9]

[rBurning:10]

[rBurning:11]

She looks at me.

[rBurning:12]

**MC:** Please don’t touch me.

[rBurning:13]

Her arm falls away.

[rBurning:14]

I leave before either of them can say anything else.

[rBurning:15]

I take the yellow envelope with me.

I tuck the envelope inside a notebook and slide it into my bag.

[rKeepSealed:6]

*They aren’t taking this one.*

[rKeepSealed:7]

I keep it.

[rKeepSealed:8]

Through the next semester. Through changing bags. Through every time I think about finding him.

[rKeepSealed:9]

After a while, opening it feels like another thing I’ve waited too long to do.

[rKeepSealed:10]

But when I pack for Saint Luis, I bring it.

**Next:** rAfterEnvelope.

## A few months ago [rReadNowAfter]

[Background: manila-bedroom; time: night.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: hidden; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[rReadNowAfter:0]

I look at the date again.

[rReadNowAfter:1]

*A few months ago.*

[rReadNowAfter:2]

*Lola was still making pancit.*

[rReadNowAfter:3]

*Still asking him to reach things.*

[rReadNowAfter:4]

*Still sending reminders through someone else’s letter.*

[rReadNowAfter:5]

I read the part about her twice.

[rReadNowAfter:6]

Then I fold the pages along the same creases.

[rReadNowAfter:7]

*He was waiting for an answer.*

[rReadNowAfter:8]

*They both were.*

[rReadNowAfter:9]

**MC:** I missed you too.

[rReadNowAfter:10]

There’s nobody here to hear it.

[rReadNowAfter:11]

I put the letter back in its envelope and keep it beside me that night.

**Next:** rAfterEnvelope.

## What I carried [rAfterEnvelope]

[Background: manila-bedroom; time: night.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: hidden; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[rAfterEnvelope:0]

After that, whenever I thought about contacting Rowan, I remembered the box.

[rAfterEnvelope:1]

I was still living with my parents. I was afraid of what else they might take away.

[rAfterEnvelope:2]

And I didn’t know how to explain what had happened.

[rAfterEnvelope:3]

So I kept putting it off.

**Next:** rWater.

## Familiar, and different [rInside]

[Background: living; time: day.]

[Music: back-together.]

[Rowan sprite: hidden; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[rInside:0]

Rowan pushes the door open.

[rInside:1]

It moves without catching.

[rInside:2]

I follow him in.

[rInside:3]

The curtains are clean. The table has been wiped down.

[rInside:4]

Lola’s old chair is still by the window.

[rInside:5]

There’s a new piece of wood beneath one armrest. It’s lighter than the rest.

[rInside:6]

**MC:** You fixed her chair.

[rInside:7]

**Rowan:** It was coming loose.

[rInside:8]

I run my thumb over the edge. He’s sanded it smooth.

[rInside:9]

**Rowan:** I’ll get some water.

[rInside:10]

He heads toward the kitchen.

[rInside:11]

I set my bag beside the chair and open the inside pocket.

[rInside:12]

My cards are still there.

[rInside:13]

So is the envelope I brought with me.

[rInside:14]

Yellow, though not as bright as it used to be.

[rInside:15]

I hadn’t planned to take it out yet.

[rInside:16]

The last time I held it like this, I was standing in my room in Manila.

## What I want to tell you [rWaterReply]

[Background: living; time: day.]

[Music: back-together.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[rWaterReply:0 · Only if letterTiming = "now"]

I glance at the envelope. The pancit. The cupboard door. Her reminder to eat.

[rWaterReply:1 · Only if letterTiming = "now"]

That was the last letter he sent while she was still here.

[rWaterReply:2 · Only if letterTiming = "now"]

**MC:** She told you to make sure I was eating.

[rWaterReply:3 · Only if letterTiming = "now"; Expression: smile]

**Rowan:** Yeah. She worried.

[rWaterReply:4 · Only if letterTiming = "now"]

**MC:** You wrote it down.

[rWaterReply:5 · Only if letterTiming = "now"]

**Rowan:** She asked me twice.

[rWaterReply:6 · Only if letterTiming = "now"]

I look down before he can see my face change.

[rWaterReply:7 · Only if letterTiming = "later"; Expression: neutral]

His eyes settle briefly on the flap.

[rWaterReply:8 · Only if letterTiming = "later"]

**MC:** I haven’t opened it.

[rWaterReply:9 · Only if letterTiming = "later"; Expression: concerned]

**Rowan:** Oh.

[rWaterReply:10 · Only if letterTiming = "later"]

**MC:** I wanted to. I just…

[rWaterReply:11 · Only if letterTiming = "later"]

I stop. He nods, though I’m not sure how much he understands.

[rWaterReply:12 · Only if letterTiming = "later"]

**Rowan:** You don’t have to read it in front of me.

[rWaterReply:13 · Only if letterTiming = "later"]

**MC:** I think I’d like to read it upstairs.

[rWaterReply:14 · Only if letterTiming = "later"; Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** Okay.

[rWaterReply:15 · Only if letterTiming = "later"]

He leaves the envelope where it is.

[rWaterReply:16]

**MC:** There’s something I need to tell you.

[rWaterReply:17]

He waits.

[rWaterReply:18]

**MC:** Not yet. But I want to.

[rWaterReply:19 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** Okay.

[rWaterReply:20]

He nudges the water closer.

[rWaterReply:21]

**Rowan:** Start with that.

[rWaterReply:22]

I take a drink. For now, I sit with him.

[Background: bedroom; time: evening.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: hidden; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[rLetterLaterAfter:0]

I glance toward the window.

[rLetterLaterAfter:1]

[rLetterLaterAfter:2]

*The things he was trying to tell me two years ago.*

[rLetterLaterAfter:3]

My eyes return to the part about Lola.

[rLetterLaterAfter:4]

[rLetterLaterAfter:5]

[rLetterLaterAfter:6]

[rLetterLaterAfter:7]

I rest the pages on my lap.

[rLetterLaterAfter:8]

For a while, I listen to the house.

[rLetterLaterAfter:9]

Then I fold them carefully and put them back.

[rLetterLaterAfter:10]

[rLetterLaterAfter:11]

[rLetterLaterAfter:12]

## The room I remember [d2Settling]

[Background: d2-bedroom-evening; time: evening; day: 1.]

[d2Settling:3]

[d2Settling:6]

[d2Settling:7]

*He’s still here.*

[d2Goodnight:9]

[d2Goodnight:10]

[d2Goodnight:11]

[d2Goodnight:12 · Expression: playful]

**Rowan:** Unless you’ve got a complaint.

[d2Goodnight:13]

[d2Goodnight:14]

His smile widens.

[d2Goodnight:15]

[d2Goodnight:16]

His footsteps retreat down the stairs.

[d2Goodnight:17]

*Sunshine. I haven’t heard him say that in so long.*

## Peace and quiet [d2FallingAsleep]

[Background: d2-bedroom-night; time: night; day: 1.]

[Music: scene default.]

[Rowan sprite: hidden; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[d2FallingAsleep:0]

I lie back. The fan turns with a faint, uneven click.

[d2FallingAsleep:1]

Outside, someone calls a child home. A gate closes.

[d2FallingAsleep:2]

*Ordinary sounds. I missed them.*

[d2FallingAsleep:3]

**MC:** I’m really here.

[d2FallingAsleep:4]

*Saint Luis. My old room.*

[d2FallingAsleep:5]

*Rowan just downstairs—or maybe already walking home.*

[d2FallingAsleep:6]

*Everything is familiar. It’s me I’m still trying to get used to.*

[d2FallingAsleep:7]

I close my eyes.

[d2FallingAsleep:8]

*Please, just for tonight. Let my mind rest.*

[d2FallingAsleep:9]

*Tomorrow can have the questions.*

[d2FallingAsleep:10]

For now, I pull the blanket over my waist and turn toward the breeze.

[d2FallingAsleep:11]

[d2FallingAsleep:12]

The fan clicks once more. I don’t hear the next one.

**Next:** d2Morning.

## A Saint Luis morning [d2Morning]

[Background: d2-bedroom-morning; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: back-together.]

[Rowan sprite: hidden; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[d2Morning:0]

A rooster announces the morning like nobody asked him to keep it down.

[d2Morning:1]

Another one joins in.

[d2Morning:2]

**MC:** All right. I’m awake.

[d2Morning:3]

I stretch beneath the blanket. The Saint Luis heat has already found me.

[d2Morning:4]

In Manila, I’d be complaining by now. Here, I stay still for another moment.

[d2Morning:5]

A breeze slips through the window. Leaves rustle outside.

[d2Morning:6]

Somewhere beyond the fence, two neighbors are discussing someone else’s business at a perfectly unreasonable volume.

[d2Morning:7]

**MC:** The neighborhood gossip briefing has started.

[d2Morning:8]

I smile into my pillow. Some things never change.

[d2Morning:9]

Eventually, my stomach convinces me to sit up. Maybe I can make breakfast.

[d2Morning:10]

I reach for my phone to check the time. My smile disappears.

[d2Morning:11]

Notifications fill the screen. Mom. Dad. Again. And again. Even on SG.

[d2Morning:12]

**MC:** How did they find this account?

[d2Morning:13]

I stare at the account names. One person comes to mind almost immediately.

[d2Morning:14]

*Sevi. He knows this account.*

[d2Morning:15]

That doesn’t prove anything. Still, my jaw tightens.

[Background: d2-bedroom-morning; time: day; day: 2.]

[d2PhoneAway:0]

## Too many notifications [d2PhoneOpen]

[Background: d2-bedroom-morning; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: lost-contact.]

[Rowan sprite: hidden; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[d2PhoneOpen:0]

*Maybe one of them just wants to know if I arrived safely.*

[d2PhoneOpen:1]

I hate how quickly I reach for that possibility. But I open the notifications anyway.

[d2PhoneOpen:2]

**Phone:** Dad — 60 missed calls. Mom — 34 missed calls.

[d2PhoneOpen:3]

I check the numbers twice. As if they might change.

[d2PhoneOpen:4]

Then I open Mom’s messages.

[d2PhoneOpen:5]

**Mom · SG:** Where are you?

[d2PhoneOpen:6]

**Mom · SG:** Answer our calls.

[d2PhoneOpen:7]

**Mom · SG:** Did you think we wouldn’t know where you were going?

[d2PhoneOpen:8]

**Mom · SG:** You are in big trouble.

[d2PhoneOpen:9]

**Mom · SG:** Why can’t you just listen to us?

[d2PhoneOpen:10]

**Mom · SG:** How could you do something so stupid?

[d2PhoneOpen:11]

There are more. I scroll. Then stop.

[d2PhoneOpen:12]

Dad’s messages aren’t much different.

[d2PhoneOpen:13]

**Dad · SG:** Answer your mother.

[d2PhoneOpen:14]

**Dad · SG:** Come home. We’ll talk about this.

[d2PhoneOpen:15]

**Dad · SG:** You can’t just leave whenever you don’t like what we say.

[d2PhoneOpen:16]

I press my lips together. So much for hoping.

[d2PhoneOpen:17]

A different name appears below theirs. Sevrine Buenaventura.

[d2PhoneOpen:18]

*Four messages. Of course.*

[d2PhoneOpen:19]

**Sevi · SG:** Hey, Dos. Where are you?

[d2PhoneOpen:20]

**Sevi · SG:** Your parents keep asking if you told me anything.

[d2PhoneOpen:21]

**Sevi · SG:** I don’t want to get involved in your family problems.

[d2PhoneOpen:22]

**Sevi · SG:** Just talk to them, okay? Bye, Dos.

[d2PhoneOpen:23]

I read the third message again. He doesn’t want to get involved. Convenient.

[d2PhoneOpen:24]

**MC:** Even here. Seriously.

[d2PhoneOpen:25]

Nothing in his messages tells me whether he gave them my account.

[d2PhoneOpen:26]

*But he’s talking as if nothing happened between us. As if he wasn’t part of the reason I finally left.*

[d2PhoneOpen:27]

My fingers tighten around the phone. For a second, I consider replying.

[d2PhoneOpen:28]

Then I close the conversation. Not this morning.

[d2PhoneOpen:29]

I silence the notifications and put the phone down.

**Next:** d2GetUp.

Rowan looks down at his hands.

[aTopic0_lola_yes:2]

**Rowan:** She started getting tired more easily. Sometimes she had to stop halfway through something and sit down.

[aTopic0_lola_yes:3]

**Rowan:** The doctor here was worried about her heart. He wanted her to see a specialist in the city.

[aTopic0_lola_yes:4]

**MC:** Did she go?

[aTopic0_lola_yes:5]

**Rowan:** No.

[aTopic0_lola_yes:6]

I wait for him to continue.

[aTopic0_lola_yes:7]

**Rowan:** She worried about the cost. About leaving the house. Every time we brought it up, she found another reason to wait.

[aTopic0_lola_yes:8]

**Rowan:** Mama offered to go with her. I offered too.

[aTopic0_lola_yes:9]

**MC:** What about my parents?

[aTopic0_lola_yes:10 · Expression: concerned]

**Rowan:** She asked them to help arrange the trip.

[aTopic0_lola_yes:11]

[aTopic0_lola_yes:12]

**Rowan:** I was there when she called. I only heard her side.

[aTopic0_lola_yes:13]

[aTopic0_lola_yes:14]

[aTopic0_lola_yes:15]

**MC:** What did they say to her?

[aTopic0_lola_yes:16]

**Rowan:** I don’t know.

[aTopic0_lola_yes:17]

[aTopic0_lola_yes:18]

He looks at me.

[aTopic0_lola_yes:19]

**Rowan:** You never heard about the specialist?

[aTopic0_lola_yes:20]

I shake my head.

**Rowan:** A few days after I wrote that yellow letter, she had a bad spell. That was when the doctor asked her to go.

[aTopic0_lola_details:2]

*The letter.*

[aTopic0_lola_details:3]

*She was still telling him what to write.*

[aTopic0_lola_details:4 · Expression: sad]

**Rowan:** There were better days after that. She’d feel stronger, and we’d think we had more time.

[aTopic0_lola_details:5]

**Rowan:** Then, a few months later, she got much worse. We took her to the hospital here.

[aTopic0_lola_details:6]

He rubs his thumb against his palm.

[aTopic0_lola_details:7]

**Rowan:** She died there.

[aTopic0_lola_details:8]

**MC:** And nobody told me how sick she was.

[aTopic0_lola_details:9]

**Rowan:** You should have been told.

## Tita Mayumi [aTopic0_mayumi_start]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic0_mayumi_start:0]

[aTopic0_mayumi_start:1 · Expression: neutral]

[aTopic0_mayumi_start:2]

**MC:** That sounds familiar.

[aTopic0_mayumi_start:3 · Expression: smile]

[aTopic0_mayumi_start:4]

[aTopic0_mayumi_start:6]

[aTopic0_mayumi_start:7]

He smiles.

[aTopic0_mayumi_start:8]

[aTopic0_mayumi_start:9]

[aTopic0_mayumi_start:10]

[aTopic0_mayumi_start:11]

[aTopic0_mayumi_start:12]

## Dos [aTopic0_school_rival]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic0_school_rival:0]

**MC:** He’s my so-called academic rival.

[aTopic0_school_rival:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** So-called?

[aTopic0_school_rival:2]

**MC:** Our classmates decided it. Now everything we do gets compared.

[aTopic0_school_rival:3]

**MC:** He calls me “Dos.”

[aTopic0_school_rival:4]

**Rowan:** As in second?

[aTopic0_school_rival:5]

**MC:** Yeah.

[aTopic0_school_rival:6 · Expression: concerned]

Rowan’s smile fades.

[aTopic0_school_rival:7]

**MC:** And he’s student council president, so there’s always a reason he can miss something.

[aTopic0_school_rival:8]

**MC:** A meeting. An event. Something important.

[aTopic0_school_rival:9]

**MC:** I know some of it is legitimate. But everyone else has to work around it.

[aTopic0_school_rival:10]

**Rowan:** And you end up doing more?

[aTopic0_school_rival:11]

**MC:** Sometimes. Or I’m expected to be fine with it.

[aTopic0_school_rival:12]

**MC:** That’s what gets me. He comes back and acts like nothing happened.

[aTopic0_school_rival:13]

**MC:** Calls me Dos. Makes another joke.

[aTopic0_school_rival:14]

I realize I’m gripping my glass. I put it down.

[aTopic0_school_rival:15]

**MC:** He’s part of why I stopped joining orgs.

[aTopic0_school_rival:16]

**MC:** And part of why I—

[aTopic0_school_rival:17]

I stop. Rowan waits.

[aTopic0_school_rival:18]

**MC:** Sorry. I didn’t mean to unload all that on you.

[aTopic0_school_rival:19]

**Rowan:** You don’t have to apologize for telling me.

[aTopic0_school_rival:20]

**MC:** Can we leave the rest for another time?

[aTopic0_school_rival:21]

**Rowan:** Of course.

[aTopic0_school_rival:22]

He leans back.

[aTopic0_school_rival:23]

**Rowan:** Do you want me to say something, or would you rather just leave it there?

[aTopic0_school_rival:24]

**MC:** Leave it there. For now.

[aTopic0_school_rival:25]

**Rowan:** Okay.

**Next:** aTopics4.

## What comes next [aTopic0_rowan_start]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic0_rowan_start:0]

**MC:** What about you?

[aTopic0_rowan_start:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** Me?

[aTopic0_rowan_start:2]

**MC:** Yeah. How are you?

[aTopic0_rowan_start:3 · Expression: smile]

**Rowan:** I’m doing okay.

[aTopic0_rowan_start:4]

He looks toward the gate. Then back at me.

[aTopic0_rowan_start:5 · Expression: embarrassed]

**Rowan:** That was a bit automatic.

[aTopic0_rowan_start:6]

**MC:** You don’t have to give me the easy answer.

[aTopic0_rowan_start:7]

He rubs the back of his neck.

[aTopic0_rowan_start:8]

**Rowan:** There’s a lot I want to tell you. I just don’t know where to start.

[aTopic0_rowan_start:9]

**MC:** We don’t have to finish today.

[aTopic0_rowan_start:10 · Expression: smile]

He notices me repeating his words. His smile comes back.

[aTopic0_rowan_start:11]

**Rowan:** Right.

[aTopic0_rowan_start:12]

**Rowan:** For now… I’m glad you’re here.

[aTopic0_rowan_start:13]

**MC:** I’m glad too.

[aTopic0_rowan_start:14]

**Rowan:** I’ve been looking after the house. Helping Mama. Working when I can.

[aTopic0_rowan_start:15]

He pauses.

[aTopic0_rowan_start:16 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** And thinking about what comes next.

[aTopic0_rowan_start:17]

**MC:** What do you want to do?

[aTopic0_rowan_start:18]

**Rowan:** I’m still figuring out how to say it without talking myself out of it.

[aTopic0_rowan_start:19]

**MC:** You can tell me when you’re ready.

[aTopic0_rowan_start:20 · Expression: smile]

**Rowan:** Thanks.

[aTopic0_rowan_start:21]

His knee bumps mine when he shifts. “Sorry,” he says, adjusting his chair.

[aTopic0_rowan_start:22]

**Rowan:** One thing hasn’t changed.

[aTopic0_rowan_start:23]

**MC:** What?

[aTopic0_rowan_start:24]

**Rowan:** I still like having you around.

[aTopic0_rowan_start:25 · Only if gardenGreeting = "whatcha"]

**MC:** Even when I complain this much?

[aTopic0_rowan_start:26 · Only if gardenGreeting = "hey"]

**MC:** Even when I complain this much?

[aTopic0_rowan_start:27 · Only if gardenGreeting = "poke"]

**MC:** Even when I complain this much?

[aTopic0_rowan_start:28 · Only if gardenGreeting = "scare"]

**MC:** Even when I scare you in the garden?

[aTopic0_rowan_start:29 · Expression: playful]

**Rowan:** I’m choosing to overlook a few things.

[aTopic0_rowan_start:30]

I laugh. He looks pleased with himself.

**Next:** aTopics8.

[aTopic1_school_rant:5]

## Dos [aTopic1_school_rival]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic1_school_rival:0]

**MC:** He’s my so-called academic rival.

[aTopic1_school_rival:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** So-called?

[aTopic1_school_rival:2]

**MC:** Our classmates decided it. Now everything we do gets compared.

[aTopic1_school_rival:3]

**MC:** He calls me “Dos.”

[aTopic1_school_rival:4]

**Rowan:** As in second?

[aTopic1_school_rival:5]

**MC:** Yeah.

[aTopic1_school_rival:6 · Expression: concerned]

Rowan’s smile fades.

[aTopic1_school_rival:7]

**MC:** And he’s student council president, so there’s always a reason he can miss something.

[aTopic1_school_rival:8]

**MC:** A meeting. An event. Something important.

[aTopic1_school_rival:9]

**MC:** I know some of it is legitimate. But everyone else has to work around it.

[aTopic1_school_rival:10]

**Rowan:** And you end up doing more?

[aTopic1_school_rival:11]

**MC:** Sometimes. Or I’m expected to be fine with it.

[aTopic1_school_rival:12]

**MC:** That’s what gets me. He comes back and acts like nothing happened.

[aTopic1_school_rival:13]

**MC:** Calls me Dos. Makes another joke.

[aTopic1_school_rival:14]

I realize I’m gripping my glass. I put it down.

[aTopic1_school_rival:15]

**MC:** He’s part of why I stopped joining orgs.

[aTopic1_school_rival:16]

**MC:** And part of why I—

[aTopic1_school_rival:17]

I stop. Rowan waits.

[aTopic1_school_rival:18]

**MC:** Sorry. I didn’t mean to unload all that on you.

[aTopic1_school_rival:19]

**Rowan:** You don’t have to apologize for telling me.

[aTopic1_school_rival:20]

**MC:** Can we leave the rest for another time?

[aTopic1_school_rival:21]

**Rowan:** Of course.

[aTopic1_school_rival:22]

He leans back.

[aTopic1_school_rival:23]

**Rowan:** Do you want me to say something, or would you rather just leave it there?

[aTopic1_school_rival:24]

**MC:** Leave it there. For now.

[aTopic1_school_rival:25]

**Rowan:** Okay.

**Next:** aTopics5.

## What comes next [aTopic1_rowan_start]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic1_rowan_start:0]

**MC:** What about you?

[aTopic1_rowan_start:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** Me?

[aTopic1_rowan_start:2]

**MC:** Yeah. How are you?

[aTopic1_rowan_start:3 · Expression: smile]

**Rowan:** I’m doing okay.

[aTopic1_rowan_start:4]

He looks toward the gate. Then back at me.

[aTopic1_rowan_start:5 · Expression: embarrassed]

**Rowan:** That was a bit automatic.

[aTopic1_rowan_start:6]

**MC:** You don’t have to give me the easy answer.

[aTopic1_rowan_start:7]

He rubs the back of his neck.

[aTopic1_rowan_start:8]

**Rowan:** There’s a lot I want to tell you. I just don’t know where to start.

[aTopic1_rowan_start:9]

**MC:** We don’t have to finish today.

[aTopic1_rowan_start:10 · Expression: smile]

He notices me repeating his words. His smile comes back.

[aTopic1_rowan_start:11]

**Rowan:** Right.

[aTopic1_rowan_start:12]

**Rowan:** For now… I’m glad you’re here.

[aTopic1_rowan_start:13]

**MC:** I’m glad too.

[aTopic1_rowan_start:14]

**Rowan:** I’ve been looking after the house. Helping Mama. Working when I can.

[aTopic1_rowan_start:15]

He pauses.

[aTopic1_rowan_start:16 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** And thinking about what comes next.

[aTopic1_rowan_start:17]

**MC:** What do you want to do?

[aTopic1_rowan_start:18]

**Rowan:** I’m still figuring out how to say it without talking myself out of it.

[aTopic1_rowan_start:19]

**MC:** You can tell me when you’re ready.

[aTopic1_rowan_start:20 · Expression: smile]

**Rowan:** Thanks.

[aTopic1_rowan_start:21]

His knee bumps mine when he shifts. “Sorry,” he says, adjusting his chair.

[aTopic1_rowan_start:22]

**Rowan:** One thing hasn’t changed.

[aTopic1_rowan_start:23]

**MC:** What?

[aTopic1_rowan_start:24]

**Rowan:** I still like having you around.

[aTopic1_rowan_start:25 · Only if gardenGreeting = "whatcha"]

**MC:** Even when I complain this much?

[aTopic1_rowan_start:26 · Only if gardenGreeting = "hey"]

**MC:** Even when I complain this much?

[aTopic1_rowan_start:27 · Only if gardenGreeting = "poke"]

**MC:** Even when I complain this much?

[aTopic1_rowan_start:28 · Only if gardenGreeting = "scare"]

**MC:** Even when I scare you in the garden?

[aTopic1_rowan_start:29 · Expression: playful]

**Rowan:** I’m choosing to overlook a few things.

[aTopic1_rowan_start:30]

I laugh. He looks pleased with himself.

**Next:** aTopics9.

## What happened [aTopic2_lola_yes]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic2_lola_yes:0]

**MC:** Yes. Please.

[aTopic2_lola_yes:1 · Expression: sad]

Rowan looks down at his hands.

[aTopic2_lola_yes:2]

**Rowan:** She started getting tired more easily. Sometimes she had to stop halfway through something and sit down.

[aTopic2_lola_yes:3]

**Rowan:** The doctor here was worried about her heart. He wanted her to see a specialist in the city.

[aTopic2_lola_yes:4]

**MC:** Did she go?

[aTopic2_lola_yes:5]

**Rowan:** No.

[aTopic2_lola_yes:6]

I wait for him to continue.

[aTopic2_lola_yes:7]

**Rowan:** She worried about the cost. About leaving the house. Every time we brought it up, she found another reason to wait.

[aTopic2_lola_yes:8]

**Rowan:** Mama offered to go with her. I offered too.

[aTopic2_lola_yes:9]

**MC:** What about my parents?

[aTopic2_lola_yes:10 · Expression: concerned]

**Rowan:** She asked them to help arrange the trip.

[aTopic2_lola_yes:11]

**MC:** You heard her?

[aTopic2_lola_yes:12]

**Rowan:** I was there when she called. I only heard her side.

[aTopic2_lola_yes:13]

He runs his thumb along the seam of his trousers.

[aTopic2_lola_yes:14]

**Rowan:** Afterward, she said she didn’t want to ask again.

[aTopic2_lola_yes:15]

**MC:** What did they say to her?

[aTopic2_lola_yes:16]

**Rowan:** I don’t know.

[aTopic2_lola_yes:17]

**MC:** They told me she was fine.

[aTopic2_lola_yes:18]

He looks at me.

[aTopic2_lola_yes:19]

**Rowan:** You never heard about the specialist?

[aTopic2_lola_yes:20]

I shake my head.

[aTopic2_lola_yes:21]

**Rowan:** I thought you knew she was ill. Just… not how bad it was getting.

[aTopic2_lola_yes:22]

I can’t think of anything to say.

[aTopic2_lola_yes:23]

**MC:** I knew none of this.

[aTopic2_lola_yes:24 · Expression: sad]

**Rowan:** I’m sorry.

[aTopic2_lola_yes:25]

He shifts, then stops.

[aTopic2_lola_yes:26]

**Rowan:** Do you want me to keep going?

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic2_school_rival:0]

**MC:** He’s my so-called academic rival.

[aTopic2_school_rival:1 · Expression: neutral]

[aTopic2_school_rival:2]

**MC:** Our classmates decided it. Now everything we do gets compared.

[aTopic2_school_rival:3]

[aTopic2_school_rival:5]

**MC:** Yeah.

[aTopic2_school_rival:6 · Expression: concerned]

Rowan’s smile fades.

[aTopic2_school_rival:7]

**MC:** And he’s student council president, so there’s always a reason he can miss something.

[aTopic2_school_rival:8]

**MC:** A meeting. An event. Something important.

[aTopic2_school_rival:9]

**MC:** I know some of it is legitimate. But everyone else has to work around it.

[aTopic2_school_rival:10]

**Rowan:** And you end up doing more?

[aTopic2_school_rival:11]

**MC:** Sometimes. Or I’m expected to be fine with it.

[aTopic2_school_rival:12]

**MC:** That’s what gets me. He comes back and acts like nothing happened.

[aTopic2_school_rival:13]

**MC:** Calls me Dos. Makes another joke.

[aTopic2_school_rival:14]

I realize I’m gripping my glass. I put it down.

[aTopic2_school_rival:15]

**MC:** He’s part of why I stopped joining orgs.

[aTopic2_school_rival:16]

**MC:** And part of why I—

[aTopic2_school_rival:17]

I stop. Rowan waits.

[aTopic2_school_rival:18]

**MC:** Sorry. I didn’t mean to unload all that on you.

[aTopic2_school_rival:19]

**Rowan:** You don’t have to apologize for telling me.

[aTopic2_school_rival:20]

**MC:** Can we leave the rest for another time?

[aTopic2_school_rival:21]

**Rowan:** Of course.

[aTopic2_school_rival:22]

He leans back.

[aTopic2_school_rival:23]

**Rowan:** Do you want me to say something, or would you rather just leave it there?

[aTopic2_school_rival:24]

**MC:** Leave it there. For now.

[aTopic2_school_rival:25]

**Rowan:** Okay.

## What comes next [aTopic2_rowan_start]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic2_rowan_start:0]

**MC:** What about you?

[aTopic2_rowan_start:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** Me?

[aTopic2_rowan_start:2]

**MC:** Yeah. How are you?

[aTopic2_rowan_start:3 · Expression: smile]

**Rowan:** I’m doing okay.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:4]

He looks toward the gate. Then back at me.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:5 · Expression: embarrassed]

**Rowan:** That was a bit automatic.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:6]

**MC:** You don’t have to give me the easy answer.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:7]

He rubs the back of his neck.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:8]

**Rowan:** There’s a lot I want to tell you. I just don’t know where to start.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:9]

**MC:** We don’t have to finish today.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:10 · Expression: smile]

He notices me repeating his words. His smile comes back.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:11]

**Rowan:** Right.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:12]

**Rowan:** For now… I’m glad you’re here.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:13]

**MC:** I’m glad too.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:14]

**Rowan:** I’ve been looking after the house. Helping Mama. Working when I can.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:15]

He pauses.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:16 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** And thinking about what comes next.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:17]

**MC:** What do you want to do?

[aTopic2_rowan_start:18]

**Rowan:** I’m still figuring out how to say it without talking myself out of it.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:19]

**MC:** You can tell me when you’re ready.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:20 · Expression: smile]

**Rowan:** Thanks.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:21]

His knee bumps mine when he shifts. “Sorry,” he says, adjusting his chair.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:22]

**Rowan:** One thing hasn’t changed.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:23]

**MC:** What?

[aTopic2_rowan_start:24]

**Rowan:** I still like having you around.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:25 · Only if gardenGreeting = "whatcha"]

**MC:** Even when I complain this much?

[aTopic2_rowan_start:26 · Only if gardenGreeting = "hey"]

**MC:** Even when I complain this much?

[aTopic2_rowan_start:27 · Only if gardenGreeting = "poke"]

**MC:** Even when I complain this much?

[aTopic2_rowan_start:28 · Only if gardenGreeting = "scare"]

**MC:** Even when I scare you in the garden?

[aTopic2_rowan_start:29 · Expression: playful]

**Rowan:** I’m choosing to overlook a few things.

[aTopic2_rowan_start:30]

I laugh. He looks pleased with himself.

## What happened [aTopic4_lola_yes]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic4_lola_yes:0]

**MC:** Yes. Please.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:1 · Expression: sad]

Rowan looks down at his hands.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:2]

**Rowan:** She started getting tired more easily. Sometimes she had to stop halfway through something and sit down.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:3]

**Rowan:** The doctor here was worried about her heart. He wanted her to see a specialist in the city.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:4]

**MC:** Did she go?

[aTopic4_lola_yes:5]

**Rowan:** No.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:6]

I wait for him to continue.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:7]

**Rowan:** She worried about the cost. About leaving the house. Every time we brought it up, she found another reason to wait.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:8]

**Rowan:** Mama offered to go with her. I offered too.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:9]

**MC:** What about my parents?

[aTopic4_lola_yes:10 · Expression: concerned]

**Rowan:** She asked them to help arrange the trip.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:11]

**MC:** You heard her?

[aTopic4_lola_yes:12]

**Rowan:** I was there when she called. I only heard her side.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:13]

He runs his thumb along the seam of his trousers.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:14]

**Rowan:** Afterward, she said she didn’t want to ask again.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:15]

**MC:** What did they say to her?

[aTopic4_lola_yes:16]

**Rowan:** I don’t know.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:17]

**MC:** They told me she was fine.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:18]

He looks at me.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:19]

**Rowan:** You never heard about the specialist?

[aTopic4_lola_yes:20]

I shake my head.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:21]

**Rowan:** I thought you knew she was ill. Just… not how bad it was getting.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:22]

I can’t think of anything to say.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:23]

**MC:** I knew none of this.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:24 · Expression: sad]

**Rowan:** I’m sorry.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:25]

He shifts, then stops.

[aTopic4_lola_yes:26]

**Rowan:** Do you want me to keep going?

## The rest of it [aTopic4_lola_details]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic4_lola_details:0]

**MC:** When did this start?

[aTopic4_lola_details:1]

**Rowan:** A few days after I wrote that yellow letter, she had a bad spell. That was when the doctor asked her to go.

[aTopic4_lola_details:2]

*The letter.*

[aTopic4_lola_details:3]

*She was still telling him what to write.*

[aTopic4_lola_details:4 · Expression: sad]

**Rowan:** There were better days after that. She’d feel stronger, and we’d think we had more time.

[aTopic4_lola_details:5]

**Rowan:** Then, a few months later, she got much worse. We took her to the hospital here.

[aTopic4_lola_details:6]

He rubs his thumb against his palm.

[aTopic4_lola_details:7]

**Rowan:** She died there.

[aTopic4_lola_details:8]

**MC:** And nobody told me how sick she was.

[aTopic4_lola_details:9]

**Rowan:** You should have been told.

## Tita Mayumi [aTopic4_mayumi_start]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic4_mayumi_start:0]

**MC:** How’s Tita Mayumi?

[aTopic4_mayumi_start:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** Busy. Still insists she isn’t.

[aTopic4_mayumi_start:2]

**MC:** That sounds familiar.

[aTopic4_mayumi_start:3 · Expression: smile]

**Rowan:** She’ll be happy you’re back.

[aTopic4_mayumi_start:4]

**MC:** I’d like to see her.

[aTopic4_mayumi_start:5]

**Rowan:** I’ll tell her. Then you’ll have to accept whatever food she decides you haven’t been eating enough of.

[aTopic4_mayumi_start:6]

**MC:** I can live with that.

[aTopic4_mayumi_start:7]

He smiles.

[aTopic4_mayumi_start:8]

**MC:** Where’s she working now?

[aTopic4_mayumi_start:9]

**Rowan:** Customer service in the next town. Calls, complaints, helping people with their accounts.

[aTopic4_mayumi_start:10]

**Rowan:** Then she comes home and somehow still has energy to ask about my day.

[aTopic4_mayumi_start:11]

**MC:** Do you help her out?

[aTopic4_mayumi_start:12]

**Rowan:** I try. Usually she tells me to finish things here first.

[aTopic4_mayumi_start:13]

He looks down at his hands.

[aTopic4_mayumi_start:14 · Expression: playful]

**Rowan:** Very unfair. All this strength, and she carries the groceries in before I reach the door.

[aTopic4_mayumi_start:15]

**MC:** Maybe she’s faster than you.

[aTopic4_mayumi_start:16]

**Rowan:** She is. Please don’t tell her I admitted it.

**Next:** aTopics6.

## What comes next [aTopic4_rowan_start]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic4_rowan_start:0]

**MC:** What about you?

[aTopic4_rowan_start:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** Me?

[aTopic4_rowan_start:2]

**MC:** Yeah. How are you?

[aTopic4_rowan_start:3 · Expression: smile]

**Rowan:** I’m doing okay.

[aTopic4_rowan_start:4]

He looks toward the gate. Then back at me.

[aTopic4_rowan_start:5 · Expression: embarrassed]

**Rowan:** That was a bit automatic.

[aTopic4_rowan_start:6]

**MC:** You don’t have to give me the easy answer.

[aTopic4_rowan_start:7]

He rubs the back of his neck.

[aTopic4_rowan_start:8]

**Rowan:** There’s a lot I want to tell you. I just don’t know where to start.

[aTopic4_rowan_start:9]

**MC:** We don’t have to finish today.

[aTopic4_rowan_start:10 · Expression: smile]

He notices me repeating his words. His smile comes back.

[aTopic4_rowan_start:11]

**Rowan:** Right.

[aTopic4_rowan_start:12]

**Rowan:** For now… I’m glad you’re here.

[aTopic4_rowan_start:13]

**MC:** I’m glad too.

[aTopic4_rowan_start:14]

**Rowan:** I’ve been looking after the house. Helping Mama. Working when I can.

[aTopic4_rowan_start:15]

He pauses.

[aTopic4_rowan_start:16 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** And thinking about what comes next.

[aTopic4_rowan_start:17]

**MC:** What do you want to do?

[aTopic4_rowan_start:18]

**Rowan:** I’m still figuring out how to say it without talking myself out of it.

[aTopic4_rowan_start:19]

**MC:** You can tell me when you’re ready.

[aTopic4_rowan_start:20 · Expression: smile]

**Rowan:** Thanks.

[aTopic4_rowan_start:21]

His knee bumps mine when he shifts. “Sorry,” he says, adjusting his chair.

[aTopic4_rowan_start:22]

**Rowan:** One thing hasn’t changed.

[aTopic4_rowan_start:23]

**MC:** What?

[aTopic4_rowan_start:24]

**Rowan:** I still like having you around.

[aTopic4_rowan_start:25 · Only if gardenGreeting = "whatcha"]

**MC:** Even when I complain this much?

[aTopic4_rowan_start:26 · Only if gardenGreeting = "hey"]

**MC:** Even when I complain this much?

[aTopic4_rowan_start:27 · Only if gardenGreeting = "poke"]

**MC:** Even when I complain this much?

[aTopic4_rowan_start:28 · Only if gardenGreeting = "scare"]

**MC:** Even when I scare you in the garden?

[aTopic4_rowan_start:29 · Expression: playful]

**Rowan:** I’m choosing to overlook a few things.

[aTopic4_rowan_start:30]

I laugh. He looks pleased with himself.

**Next:** aTopics12.

## What happened [aTopic6_lola_yes]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic6_lola_yes:0]

**MC:** Yes. Please.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:1 · Expression: sad]

Rowan looks down at his hands.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:2]

**Rowan:** She started getting tired more easily. Sometimes she had to stop halfway through something and sit down.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:3]

**Rowan:** The doctor here was worried about her heart. He wanted her to see a specialist in the city.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:4]

**MC:** Did she go?

[aTopic6_lola_yes:5]

**Rowan:** No.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:6]

I wait for him to continue.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:7]

**Rowan:** She worried about the cost. About leaving the house. Every time we brought it up, she found another reason to wait.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:8]

**Rowan:** Mama offered to go with her. I offered too.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:9]

**MC:** What about my parents?

[aTopic6_lola_yes:10 · Expression: concerned]

**Rowan:** She asked them to help arrange the trip.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:11]

**MC:** You heard her?

[aTopic6_lola_yes:12]

**Rowan:** I was there when she called. I only heard her side.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:13]

He runs his thumb along the seam of his trousers.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:14]

**Rowan:** Afterward, she said she didn’t want to ask again.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:15]

**MC:** What did they say to her?

[aTopic6_lola_yes:16]

**Rowan:** I don’t know.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:17]

**MC:** They told me she was fine.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:18]

He looks at me.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:19]

**Rowan:** You never heard about the specialist?

[aTopic6_lola_yes:20]

I shake my head.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:21]

**Rowan:** I thought you knew she was ill. Just… not how bad it was getting.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:22]

I can’t think of anything to say.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:23]

**MC:** I knew none of this.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:24 · Expression: sad]

**Rowan:** I’m sorry.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:25]

He shifts, then stops.

[aTopic6_lola_yes:26]

**Rowan:** Do you want me to keep going?

## The rest of it [aTopic6_lola_details]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic6_lola_details:0]

**MC:** When did this start?

[aTopic6_lola_details:1]

**Rowan:** A few days after I wrote that yellow letter, she had a bad spell. That was when the doctor asked her to go.

[aTopic6_lola_details:2]

*The letter.*

[aTopic6_lola_details:3]

*She was still telling him what to write.*

[aTopic6_lola_details:4 · Expression: sad]

**Rowan:** There were better days after that. She’d feel stronger, and we’d think we had more time.

[aTopic6_lola_details:5]

**Rowan:** Then, a few months later, she got much worse. We took her to the hospital here.

[aTopic6_lola_details:6]

He rubs his thumb against his palm.

[aTopic6_lola_details:7]

**Rowan:** She died there.

[aTopic6_lola_details:8]

**MC:** And nobody told me how sick she was.

[aTopic6_lola_details:9]

**Rowan:** You should have been told.

## What comes next [aTopic6_rowan_start]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic6_rowan_start:0]

**MC:** What about you?

[aTopic6_rowan_start:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** Me?

[aTopic6_rowan_start:2]

**MC:** Yeah. How are you?

[aTopic6_rowan_start:3 · Expression: smile]

**Rowan:** I’m doing okay.

[aTopic6_rowan_start:4]

He looks toward the gate. Then back at me.

[aTopic6_rowan_start:5 · Expression: embarrassed]

**Rowan:** That was a bit automatic.

[aTopic6_rowan_start:6]

**MC:** You don’t have to give me the easy answer.

[aTopic6_rowan_start:7]

He rubs the back of his neck.

[aTopic6_rowan_start:8]

**Rowan:** There’s a lot I want to tell you. I just don’t know where to start.

[aTopic6_rowan_start:9]

**MC:** We don’t have to finish today.

[aTopic6_rowan_start:10 · Expression: smile]

He notices me repeating his words. His smile comes back.

[aTopic6_rowan_start:11]

**Rowan:** Right.

[aTopic6_rowan_start:12]

**Rowan:** For now… I’m glad you’re here.

[aTopic6_rowan_start:13]

**MC:** I’m glad too.

[aTopic6_rowan_start:14]

**Rowan:** I’ve been looking after the house. Helping Mama. Working when I can.

[aTopic6_rowan_start:15]

He pauses.

[aTopic6_rowan_start:16 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** And thinking about what comes next.

[aTopic6_rowan_start:17]

**MC:** What do you want to do?

[aTopic6_rowan_start:18]

**Rowan:** I’m still figuring out how to say it without talking myself out of it.

[aTopic6_rowan_start:19]

**MC:** You can tell me when you’re ready.

[aTopic6_rowan_start:20 · Expression: smile]

**Rowan:** Thanks.

[aTopic6_rowan_start:21]

His knee bumps mine when he shifts. “Sorry,” he says, adjusting his chair.

[aTopic6_rowan_start:22]

**Rowan:** One thing hasn’t changed.

[aTopic6_rowan_start:23]

**MC:** What?

[aTopic6_rowan_start:24]

**Rowan:** I still like having you around.

[aTopic6_rowan_start:25 · Only if gardenGreeting = "whatcha"]

**MC:** Even when I complain this much?

[aTopic6_rowan_start:26 · Only if gardenGreeting = "hey"]

**MC:** Even when I complain this much?

[aTopic6_rowan_start:27 · Only if gardenGreeting = "poke"]

**MC:** Even when I complain this much?

[aTopic6_rowan_start:28 · Only if gardenGreeting = "scare"]

**MC:** Even when I scare you in the garden?

[aTopic6_rowan_start:29 · Expression: playful]

**Rowan:** I’m choosing to overlook a few things.

[aTopic6_rowan_start:30]

I laugh. He looks pleased with himself.

**Next:** aTopics14.

[aTopic8_lola_details:9]

## Tita Mayumi [aTopic8_mayumi_start]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic8_mayumi_start:0]

**MC:** How’s Tita Mayumi?

[aTopic8_mayumi_start:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** Busy. Still insists she isn’t.

[aTopic8_mayumi_start:2]

**MC:** That sounds familiar.

[aTopic8_mayumi_start:3 · Expression: smile]

**Rowan:** She’ll be happy you’re back.

[aTopic8_mayumi_start:4]

**MC:** I’d like to see her.

[aTopic8_mayumi_start:5]

[aTopic8_mayumi_start:6]

**MC:** I can live with that.

[aTopic8_mayumi_start:7]

He smiles.

[aTopic8_mayumi_start:8]

**MC:** Where’s she working now?

[aTopic8_mayumi_start:9]

[aTopic8_mayumi_start:10]

[aTopic8_mayumi_start:11]

**MC:** Do you help her out?

[aTopic8_mayumi_start:12]

[aTopic8_mayumi_start:13]

**Rowan:** She is. Please don’t tell her I admitted it.

## Dos [aTopic8_school_rival]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic8_school_rival:0]

**MC:** He’s my so-called academic rival.

[aTopic8_school_rival:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** So-called?

[aTopic8_school_rival:2]

**MC:** Our classmates decided it. Now everything we do gets compared.

[aTopic8_school_rival:3]

**MC:** He calls me “Dos.”

[aTopic8_school_rival:4]

**Rowan:** As in second?

[aTopic8_school_rival:5]

**MC:** Yeah.

[aTopic8_school_rival:6 · Expression: concerned]

Rowan’s smile fades.

[aTopic8_school_rival:7]

**MC:** And he’s student council president, so there’s always a reason he can miss something.

[aTopic8_school_rival:8]

**MC:** A meeting. An event. Something important.

[aTopic8_school_rival:9]

**MC:** I know some of it is legitimate. But everyone else has to work around it.

[aTopic8_school_rival:10]

**Rowan:** And you end up doing more?

[aTopic8_school_rival:11]

**MC:** Sometimes. Or I’m expected to be fine with it.

[aTopic8_school_rival:12]

**MC:** That’s what gets me. He comes back and acts like nothing happened.

[aTopic8_school_rival:13]

**MC:** Calls me Dos. Makes another joke.

[aTopic8_school_rival:14]

I realize I’m gripping my glass. I put it down.

[aTopic8_school_rival:15]

**MC:** He’s part of why I stopped joining orgs.

[aTopic8_school_rival:16]

**MC:** And part of why I—

[aTopic8_school_rival:17]

I stop. Rowan waits.

[aTopic8_school_rival:18]

**MC:** Sorry. I didn’t mean to unload all that on you.

[aTopic8_school_rival:19]

**Rowan:** You don’t have to apologize for telling me.

[aTopic8_school_rival:20]

**MC:** Can we leave the rest for another time?

[aTopic8_school_rival:21]

**Rowan:** Of course.

[aTopic8_school_rival:22]

He leans back.

[aTopic8_school_rival:23]

**Rowan:** Do you want me to say something, or would you rather just leave it there?

[aTopic8_school_rival:24]

**MC:** Leave it there. For now.

[aTopic8_school_rival:25]

**Rowan:** Okay.

**Next:** aTopics12.

[aTopic9_school_rival:2]

**MC:** Our classmates decided it. Now everything we do gets compared.

[aTopic9_school_rival:3]

**MC:** He calls me “Dos.”

[aTopic9_school_rival:4]

**Rowan:** As in second?

[aTopic9_school_rival:5]

**MC:** Yeah.

[aTopic9_school_rival:6 · Expression: concerned]

Rowan’s smile fades.

[aTopic9_school_rival:7]

**MC:** And he’s student council president, so there’s always a reason he can miss something.

[aTopic9_school_rival:8]

**MC:** A meeting. An event. Something important.

[aTopic9_school_rival:9]

**MC:** I know some of it is legitimate. But everyone else has to work around it.

[aTopic9_school_rival:10]

**Rowan:** And you end up doing more?

[aTopic9_school_rival:11]

**MC:** Sometimes. Or I’m expected to be fine with it.

[aTopic9_school_rival:12]

**MC:** That’s what gets me. He comes back and acts like nothing happened.

[aTopic9_school_rival:13]

**MC:** Calls me Dos. Makes another joke.

[aTopic9_school_rival:14]

I realize I’m gripping my glass. I put it down.

[aTopic9_school_rival:15]

**MC:** He’s part of why I stopped joining orgs.

[aTopic9_school_rival:16]

**MC:** And part of why I—

[aTopic9_school_rival:17]

I stop. Rowan waits.

[aTopic9_school_rival:18]

**MC:** Sorry. I didn’t mean to unload all that on you.

[aTopic9_school_rival:19]

**Rowan:** You don’t have to apologize for telling me.

[aTopic9_school_rival:20]

**MC:** Can we leave the rest for another time?

[aTopic9_school_rival:21]

**Rowan:** Of course.

[aTopic9_school_rival:22]

He leans back.

[aTopic9_school_rival:23]

**Rowan:** Do you want me to say something, or would you rather just leave it there?

[aTopic9_school_rival:24]

**MC:** Leave it there. For now.

[aTopic9_school_rival:25]

**Rowan:** Okay.

**Next:** aTopics13.

## What happened [aTopic10_lola_yes]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic10_lola_yes:0]

**MC:** Yes. Please.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:1 · Expression: sad]

Rowan looks down at his hands.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:2]

**Rowan:** She started getting tired more easily. Sometimes she had to stop halfway through something and sit down.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:3]

**Rowan:** The doctor here was worried about her heart. He wanted her to see a specialist in the city.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:4]

**MC:** Did she go?

[aTopic10_lola_yes:5]

**Rowan:** No.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:6]

I wait for him to continue.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:7]

**Rowan:** She worried about the cost. About leaving the house. Every time we brought it up, she found another reason to wait.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:8]

**Rowan:** Mama offered to go with her. I offered too.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:9]

**MC:** What about my parents?

[aTopic10_lola_yes:10 · Expression: concerned]

**Rowan:** She asked them to help arrange the trip.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:11]

**MC:** You heard her?

[aTopic10_lola_yes:12]

**Rowan:** I was there when she called. I only heard her side.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:13]

He runs his thumb along the seam of his trousers.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:14]

**Rowan:** Afterward, she said she didn’t want to ask again.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:15]

**MC:** What did they say to her?

[aTopic10_lola_yes:16]

**Rowan:** I don’t know.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:17]

**MC:** They told me she was fine.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:18]

He looks at me.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:19]

**Rowan:** You never heard about the specialist?

[aTopic10_lola_yes:20]

I shake my head.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:21]

**Rowan:** I thought you knew she was ill. Just… not how bad it was getting.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:22]

I can’t think of anything to say.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:23]

**MC:** I knew none of this.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:24 · Expression: sad]

**Rowan:** I’m sorry.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:25]

He shifts, then stops.

[aTopic10_lola_yes:26]

**Rowan:** Do you want me to keep going?

### Player choices

[aTopic11_school_brief:2 · Expression: concerned]

[aTopic11_school_brief:3]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic11_school_rant:0]

## Dos [aTopic11_school_rival]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic11_school_rival:0]

**MC:** He’s my so-called academic rival.

[aTopic11_school_rival:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** So-called?

[aTopic11_school_rival:2]

**MC:** Our classmates decided it. Now everything we do gets compared.

[aTopic11_school_rival:3]

**MC:** He calls me “Dos.”

[aTopic11_school_rival:4]

**Rowan:** As in second?

[aTopic11_school_rival:5]

**MC:** Yeah.

[aTopic11_school_rival:6 · Expression: concerned]

Rowan’s smile fades.

[aTopic11_school_rival:7]

**MC:** And he’s student council president, so there’s always a reason he can miss something.

[aTopic11_school_rival:8]

**MC:** A meeting. An event. Something important.

[aTopic11_school_rival:9]

**MC:** I know some of it is legitimate. But everyone else has to work around it.

[aTopic11_school_rival:10]

**Rowan:** And you end up doing more?

[aTopic11_school_rival:11]

**MC:** Sometimes. Or I’m expected to be fine with it.

[aTopic11_school_rival:12]

**MC:** That’s what gets me. He comes back and acts like nothing happened.

[aTopic11_school_rival:13]

**MC:** Calls me Dos. Makes another joke.

[aTopic11_school_rival:14]

I realize I’m gripping my glass. I put it down.

[aTopic11_school_rival:15]

**MC:** He’s part of why I stopped joining orgs.

[aTopic11_school_rival:16]

**MC:** And part of why I—

[aTopic11_school_rival:17]

I stop. Rowan waits.

[aTopic11_school_rival:18]

**MC:** Sorry. I didn’t mean to unload all that on you.

[aTopic11_school_rival:19]

**Rowan:** You don’t have to apologize for telling me.

[aTopic11_school_rival:20]

**MC:** Can we leave the rest for another time?

[aTopic11_school_rival:21]

**Rowan:** Of course.

[aTopic11_school_rival:22]

He leans back.

[aTopic11_school_rival:23]

**Rowan:** Do you want me to say something, or would you rather just leave it there?

[aTopic11_school_rival:24]

**MC:** Leave it there. For now.

[aTopic11_school_rival:25]

**Rowan:** Okay.

**Next:** aTopics15.

## Ask about Lola [aTopic12_lola_start]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic12_lola_start:0]

**MC:** Ro?

[aTopic12_lola_start:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** Hmm?

[aTopic12_lola_start:2]

**MC:** Can I ask about Lola?

[aTopic12_lola_start:3]

He turns toward me.

[aTopic12_lola_start:4]

**MC:** My parents told me she died. They barely told me anything else.

[aTopic12_lola_start:5]

**MC:** What happened?

[aTopic12_lola_start:6 · Expression: concerned]

**Rowan:** I can tell you what I know.

[aTopic12_lola_start:7]

He pauses.

[aTopic12_lola_start:8]

**Rowan:** Do you want to talk about it now?

### Player choices

**Choice 1 [aTopic12_lola_start:choice:0]**

Yes. I want to know.

## What happened [aTopic12_lola_yes]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic12_lola_yes:0]

**MC:** Yes. Please.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:1 · Expression: sad]

Rowan looks down at his hands.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:2]

**Rowan:** She started getting tired more easily. Sometimes she had to stop halfway through something and sit down.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:3]

**Rowan:** The doctor here was worried about her heart. He wanted her to see a specialist in the city.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:4]

**MC:** Did she go?

[aTopic12_lola_yes:5]

**Rowan:** No.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:6]

I wait for him to continue.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:7]

**Rowan:** She worried about the cost. About leaving the house. Every time we brought it up, she found another reason to wait.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:8]

**Rowan:** Mama offered to go with her. I offered too.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:9]

**MC:** What about my parents?

[aTopic12_lola_yes:10 · Expression: concerned]

**Rowan:** She asked them to help arrange the trip.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:11]

**MC:** You heard her?

[aTopic12_lola_yes:12]

**Rowan:** I was there when she called. I only heard her side.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:13]

He runs his thumb along the seam of his trousers.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:14]

**Rowan:** Afterward, she said she didn’t want to ask again.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:15]

**MC:** What did they say to her?

[aTopic12_lola_yes:16]

**Rowan:** I don’t know.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:17]

**MC:** They told me she was fine.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:18]

He looks at me.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:19]

**Rowan:** You never heard about the specialist?

[aTopic12_lola_yes:20]

I shake my head.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:21]

**Rowan:** I thought you knew she was ill. Just… not how bad it was getting.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:22]

I can’t think of anything to say.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:23]

**MC:** I knew none of this.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:24 · Expression: sad]

**Rowan:** I’m sorry.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:25]

He shifts, then stops.

[aTopic12_lola_yes:26]

**Rowan:** Do you want me to keep going?

### Player choices

**Choice 1 [aTopic12_lola_yes:choice:0]**

“Yes. Tell me.”

[Continue at: aTopic12_lola_details.]

**Choice 2 [aTopic12_lola_yes:choice:1]**

“I need a minute.”

[Continue at: aTopic12_lola_pause.]

## The rest of it [aTopic12_lola_details]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic12_lola_details:0]

**MC:** When did this start?

[aTopic12_lola_details:1]

**Rowan:** A few days after I wrote that yellow letter, she had a bad spell. That was when the doctor asked her to go.

[aTopic12_lola_details:2]

*The letter.*

[aTopic12_lola_details:3]

[aTopic12_lola_details:4 · Expression: sad]

[aTopic12_lola_details:5]

[aTopic12_lola_details:6]

He rubs his thumb against his palm.

**Rowan:** She died there.

[aTopic12_lola_details:8]

[aTopic12_lola_details:9]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:0]

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:2]

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:3 · Expression: smile]

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:7]

He smiles.

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:8]

**MC:** Where’s she working now?

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:9]

**Rowan:** Customer service in the next town. Calls, complaints, helping people with their accounts.

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:10]

**Rowan:** Then she comes home and somehow still has energy to ask about my day.

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:11]

**MC:** Do you help her out?

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:12]

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:13]

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:14 · Expression: playful]

[aTopic12_mayumi_start:16]

**Rowan:** She is. Please don’t tell her I admitted it.

## Tita Mayumi [aTopic13_mayumi_start]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: catching-up.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic13_mayumi_start:0]

**MC:** How’s Tita Mayumi?

[aTopic13_mayumi_start:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** Busy. Still insists she isn’t.

[aTopic13_mayumi_start:2]

**MC:** That sounds familiar.

[aTopic13_mayumi_start:3 · Expression: smile]

**Rowan:** She’ll be happy you’re back.

[aTopic13_mayumi_start:4]

**MC:** I’d like to see her.

[aTopic13_mayumi_start:5]

**Rowan:** I’ll tell her. Then you’ll have to accept whatever food she decides you haven’t been eating enough of.

[aTopic13_mayumi_start:6]

**MC:** I can live with that.

[aTopic13_mayumi_start:7]

He smiles.

[aTopic13_mayumi_start:8]

**MC:** Where’s she working now?

[aTopic13_mayumi_start:9]

**Rowan:** Customer service in the next town. Calls, complaints, helping people with their accounts.

[aTopic13_mayumi_start:10]

**Rowan:** Then she comes home and somehow still has energy to ask about my day.

[aTopic13_mayumi_start:11]

**MC:** Do you help her out?

[aTopic13_mayumi_start:12]

**Rowan:** I try. Usually she tells me to finish things here first.

[aTopic13_mayumi_start:13]

He looks down at his hands.

[aTopic13_mayumi_start:14 · Expression: playful]

**Rowan:** Very unfair. All this strength, and she carries the groceries in before I reach the door.

[aTopic13_mayumi_start:15]

**MC:** Maybe she’s faster than you.

[aTopic13_mayumi_start:16]

**Rowan:** She is. Please don’t tell her I admitted it.

**Next:** aTopics15.

## Ask about Lola [aTopic14_lola_start]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic14_lola_start:0]

**MC:** Ro?

[aTopic14_lola_start:1 · Expression: neutral]

**Rowan:** Hmm?

[aTopic14_lola_start:2]

**MC:** Can I ask about Lola?

[aTopic14_lola_start:3]

He turns toward me.

[aTopic14_lola_start:4]

**MC:** My parents told me she died. They barely told me anything else.

[aTopic14_lola_start:5]

**MC:** What happened?

[aTopic14_lola_start:6 · Expression: concerned]

**Rowan:** I can tell you what I know.

[aTopic14_lola_start:7]

He pauses.

[aTopic14_lola_start:8]

**Rowan:** Do you want to talk about it now?

### Player choices

**Choice 1 [aTopic14_lola_start:choice:0]**

Yes. I want to know.

[Continue at: aTopic14_lola_yes. Saved choice / relationship flags: {"porchLola":"heard"}.]

**Choice 2 [aTopic14_lola_start:choice:1]**

Not yet. I thought I was ready.

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic14_lola_notYet:0]

[aTopic14_lola_notYet:1 · Expression: concerned]

## What happened [aTopic14_lola_yes]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic14_lola_yes:0]

**MC:** Yes. Please.

[aTopic14_lola_yes:1 · Expression: sad]

Rowan looks down at his hands.

[aTopic14_lola_yes:2]

**Rowan:** She started getting tired more easily. Sometimes she had to stop halfway through something and sit down.

[aTopic14_lola_yes:3]

**Rowan:** The doctor here was worried about her heart. He wanted her to see a specialist in the city.

[aTopic14_lola_yes:4]

**MC:** Did she go?

[aTopic14_lola_yes:5]

**Rowan:** No.

[aTopic14_lola_yes:6]

I wait for him to continue.

[aTopic14_lola_yes:7]

**Rowan:** She worried about the cost. About leaving the house. Every time we brought it up, she found another reason to wait.

[aTopic14_lola_yes:8]

**Rowan:** Mama offered to go with her. I offered too.

[aTopic14_lola_yes:9]

**MC:** What about my parents?

[aTopic14_lola_yes:10 · Expression: concerned]

**Rowan:** She asked them to help arrange the trip.

[aTopic14_lola_yes:11]

**MC:** You heard her?

[aTopic14_lola_yes:12]

**Rowan:** I was there when she called. I only heard her side.

[aTopic14_lola_yes:13]

He runs his thumb along the seam of his trousers.

[aTopic14_lola_yes:14]

**Rowan:** Afterward, she said she didn’t want to ask again.

[aTopic14_lola_yes:15]

**MC:** What did they say to her?

[aTopic14_lola_yes:16]

**Rowan:** I don’t know.

[aTopic14_lola_yes:17]

**MC:** They told me she was fine.

[aTopic14_lola_yes:18]

He looks at me.

[aTopic14_lola_yes:19]

**Rowan:** You never heard about the specialist?

[aTopic14_lola_yes:20]

I shake my head.

[aTopic14_lola_yes:21]

**Rowan:** I thought you knew she was ill. Just… not how bad it was getting.

[aTopic14_lola_yes:22]

I can’t think of anything to say.

[aTopic14_lola_yes:23]

**MC:** I knew none of this.

[aTopic14_lola_yes:24 · Expression: sad]

**Rowan:** I’m sorry.

[aTopic14_lola_yes:25]

He shifts, then stops.

[aTopic14_lola_yes:26]

**Rowan:** Do you want me to keep going?

### Player choices

**Choice 1 [aTopic14_lola_yes:choice:0]**

“Yes. Tell me.”

[Continue at: aTopic14_lola_details.]

**Choice 2 [aTopic14_lola_yes:choice:1]**

“I need a minute.”

[Continue at: aTopic14_lola_pause.]

**Choice 3 [aTopic14_lola_yes:choice:2]**

“Not today.”

[Continue at: aTopic14_lola_stop.]

## The rest of it [aTopic14_lola_details]

[Background: reunion-porch; time: day; day: 2.]

[Music: letters.]

[Rowan sprite: adult; CGs take precedence while visible.]

[aTopic14_lola_details:0]

**MC:** When did this start?

[aTopic14_lola_details:1]

**Rowan:** A few days after I wrote that yellow letter, she had a bad spell. That was when the doctor asked her to go.

[aTopic14_lola_details:2]

*The letter.*

[aTopic14_lola_details:3]

*She was still telling him what to write.*

[aTopic14_lola_details:4 · Expression: sad]

**Rowan:** There were better days after that. She’d feel stronger, and we’d think we had more time.

[aTopic14_lola_details:5]

**Rowan:** Then, a few months later, she got much worse. We took her to the hospital here.

[aTopic14_lola_details:6]

He rubs his thumb against his palm.

[aTopic14_lola_details:7]

**Rowan:** She died there.

[aTopic14_lola_details:8]

**MC:** And nobody told me how sick she was.

[aTopic14_lola_details:9]

**Rowan:** You should have been told.

## Appendix — additional story text

### Rowan’s letter

[Rowan’s letter:0]

Hey, {name}.

[Rowan’s letter:1]

I turned twenty-one last week.

[Rowan’s letter:2]

Lola made pancit. She said the extra serving was for you, then told me to finish it before it went cold.

[Rowan’s letter:3]

I did. Sorry.

[Rowan’s letter:4]

I’m still not sure whether these letters are getting to you. Your parents said you’ve been busy with school.

[Rowan’s letter:5]

I hope it’s going okay.

[Rowan’s letter:6]

A few updates from here:

[Rowan’s letter:7]

I’m taller now. Properly taller. Lola asks me to get things from the top shelf instead of dragging a chair over.

[Rowan’s letter:8]

She still tells me to be careful, though. Apparently reaching for a jar is dangerous work.

[Rowan’s letter:9]

And my hair’s long.

[Rowan’s letter:10]

I meant to get it cut a while ago, but I kept putting it off. Now I can tie it back.

[Rowan’s letter:11]

Lola says it’s fine as long as I keep it out of the food.

[Rowan’s letter:12]

You’d probably have something to say about it.

[Rowan’s letter:13]

I’ve been helping her around the house. Mostly little repairs. I fixed a cupboard door last week, and it only took two tries.

[Rowan’s letter:14]

She made sure to tell your mom about the first try.

[Rowan’s letter:15]

Some things here haven’t changed much.

[Rowan’s letter:16]

Your seat at the table still wobbles.

[Rowan’s letter:17]

The hill still feels worse going up.

[Rowan’s letter:18]

I still look over when someone stops outside the gate.

[Rowan’s letter:19]

I miss you.

[Rowan’s letter:20]

I was going to write something less awkward, but that’s what I wanted to say.

[Rowan’s letter:21]

Sometimes something happens and I think, “I need to tell {name}.”

[Rowan’s letter:22]

Then I remember I can’t just go next door and find you.

[Rowan’s letter:23]

You don’t have to explain everything if you write back.

[Rowan’s letter:24]

Tell me what you had for lunch. Tell me something annoying that happened in class.

[Rowan’s letter:25]

One small thing is enough.

[Rowan’s letter:26]

I’d just like to hear from you.

[Rowan’s letter:27]

Lola says to eat properly and stop staying up so late.

[Rowan’s letter:28]

That last part might have been for both of us.

[Rowan’s letter:29]

Take care, okay?

[Rowan’s letter:30]

Ro

[Rowan’s letter:31]

P.S. The drawing is still a boat.
